# Agents · T · part 11

1,039 entries, test-runner to test-writer. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **test-runner**: Use PROACTIVELY when (a) a new REQ-ID needs a failing test before implementation (Red phase), (b) tests need running and failures triaged, or (c) coverage of a module needs verifying. Writes Dart/Flutter tests, runs them, reports failures with a root-cause hypothesis. Does NOT modify production code. ([Lioserious/ai-coding-assignment](https://github.com/Lioserious/ai-coding-assignment/blob/HEAD/.claude/agents/test-runner.md))
